**2026-10-13 own-Travel pricing outcomes — PR PENDING, NO PRICE CHANGE:** Source-led research confirms /travel/prepare already records its server-priced all-in Total, class, route distance and status, while /ops already reads a bounded recent 24-hour sample of up to 120 Travels. Candidate now uses only those already-read, protected records to show county/route-distance booking and completion/cancellation samples, with recorded and completed medians, exclusions, saturation and explicit warnings that these are neither full quote conversions nor independent competitor prices. **2026-10-13 founder reaffirmation — PR PENDING, SETTLED BUSINESS POLICY; DISTINCT LEGAL QUESTION:** American Rider's selected insurance purchasing model is **Operator-funded, independently verified commercial livery/for-hire passenger transportation coverage qualifying for each state's TNC requirements**. The app provides state-specific broker instructions at onboarding, including separate logged-on and prearranged-ride requirements; Florida is configured and unknown jurisdictions fail closed. Known expiry, cancellation, insufficient coverage, removal of the insured vehicle or failed verification prevents commencement, new requests and acceptance, with continuing independent checks and escalation. The founder considers prompt disconnection a sufficient practical defense against lapses; a genuinely offline Operator's independent private use is not represented as an American Rider Travel. The founder considers a lapse mid-Travel unlikely; that is a risk judgment, NOT proof it cannot occur. This **business/engineering model is settled and is not to be repeatedly redesigned or restated as an open preference**. HOWEVER the 2026 Florida Statutes §627.748(7)(d) expressly mentions TNC-maintained first-dollar fallback and defense if Operator coverage lapses or is insufficient during periods governed by (b) or (c); this narrow external legal question is unresolved and cannot be declared waived by software lockout. Maintain the existing company-insurance admission evidence gate until a qualified Florida coverage opinion/binding answer; do not purchase insurance, lift the gate or claim legal clearance without authorization. On pricing, prioritize one nationally reusable price-learning system: **our own quoted prices and booking outcomes**, supplemented by **consented voluntary Operator price observations** and independently lawful public comparisons. Optimize competitive local value and sustainable economics, not an inflexible discount. Keep self-reports research-only until reliability, source independence, privacy and economic holdouts pass; paid competitor APIs are not the default. Founder authorizes ongoing **Render read-only inspection/verification** in the existing 'My Workspace'; production-changing actions retain ordinary release/consent safeguards. As of this review, Render web/preview live at `c7a6dc5`, backend service last live at `efbf2869` because PR #168 changed no backend runtime file. The new review branch `fix/pricing-source-bounds-failure-retry-20261012` corrects two real source reliability gaps without touching prices: missing/deceptive Content-Length previously allowed `response.text()` to allocate arbitrarily large payloads before the announced size check, and one failed public check suppressed retries for seven days. **2026-10-12 South Florida price research source watch — PR UNDER REVIEW, NO PRICE PROMOTION:** Founder directed automatic Tri-County market-price comparisons at minimal cost for national expansion. Source audit found Miami-Dade's official passenger taxi-rate page says **$0.40** per sixth-mile after one mile, while its later 2025 legislative analysis says current rate **$0.55**; neither is a contemporary TNC upfront all-in quote. The existing market-reference engine correctly needs independent, route/time/class-matched actual consumer Totals and holds 90%-of-market promotion until holdout/economics/router evidence is available. There is no lawful unattended South Florida TNC quote feed configured; Chicago is the only active public TNC-trip collector. Also added **OPTIONAL rights-gated automatic ordinary TNC quote ingestion** via `backend/south-florida-authorized-panel.js` for three distinct county market IDs. The collector registers only after explicit approved-use authorization, immutable approval reference, an approved HTTPS source URL and a read-only supplier token are configured; it fetches at most 50 ordinary full-Total, nonpromotional, nonpersonalized, fresh observations per county and retains existing multi-family evidence promotion gates. **No approved live supplier or credential is connected in this PR; no real TNC quotes are currently collected from that path.** Synthetic tests assert three-county scope, nonpromotion, privacy, failed provider, staleness and rights refusal. The approved-panel path now requires stable anonymous observation IDs; two independent suppliers at the same time/price remain distinct, while replaying the same ID with different monetary facts fails closed. Historical public dataset document IDs are preserved. Additional two-pass tests added. Proposed **bounded WEEKLY official-rate watch** in `backend/south-florida-public-rates.js` fetches only the two fixed county government pages (zero fee, no login, redirect disallowed, 300kB/8s bounds), reconciles or flags source disagreement, stores a single minimal audit checkpoint per week in `market_public_rate_watch/fl-miami-dade`, and never changes market pricing or admission. Broward and Palm Beach remain not commissioned rather than inheriting Miami's tariff. Scheduled using existing daily leased `marketReference` cycle, with synthetic repeat, source-drift, failure, quota and nonpromotion tests. This is **public taxi context only**, *not automated verified TNC market-price collection*; a suitable permitted TNC quote/audit feed and evidence remain essential to demonstrate a claimed 10% discount. Academic/software guidance: official county sources, NBER 2025 matched-trip price study, Google Firestore billed reads, Amazon idempotency, NASA SWE-055, HBS/Stanford and internal Council. [Source audit and limitations](docs/audits/SOUTH-FLORIDA-PUBLIC-PRICE-WATCH-2026-10-12.md). Do not claim CI/merge/deploy until exact-head checks pass. No live fare, market, insurance, Stripe or passenger changes.

**2026-10-12 founder position on insurance lapses; Tri-County-first national launch; pricing and refunds — DECISION NOTE (NO LAUNCH AUTHORIZATION):** The founder reiterates that Operators are responsible for procuring and paying for qualifying insurance; the platform must independently check it and immediately block commencement of operations, new Travel requests and dispatch when coverage lapses, expires or is deficient. The founder believes that reliable continuous checks will make an unnoticed mid-Travel lapse unlikely, and considers an incident after an Operator has actually gone offline and ceased American Rider service to be outside American Rider Travel. The founder's legal interpretation is that automatic removal when a lapse is identified should satisfy the TNC's insurance obligations, without American Rider purchasing its own insurance. **This is the founder's position, NOT verified legal clearance.** The authoritative **2026 Florida Statutes §627.748(7)(a)-(d),(g) and (18)** distinguish logged-on, accepted and in-progress Travel, and explicitly require TNC-maintained coverage to respond from the first dollar and defend a claim if Operator coverage in those periods lapses or is inadequate. A software block is a necessary preventive control but does not itself displace that statutory wording; definitive no-TNC-coverage launch treatment requires written qualified Florida insurance counsel/broker/carrier analysis. Preserve Operator-funded policy and hard insurance lapse locks; do not purchase coverage or change live rules merely to record this discussion. Source: https://www.leg.state.fl.us/statutes/index.cfm?App_mode=Display_Statute&URL=0600-0699/0627/Sections/0627.748.html . Founder wishes to **launch Tri-County (Miami-Dade, Broward, Palm Beach) immediately** on one nationally reusable platform, without first commissioning all 50 states. Code `regions.js` currently lists the three counties in `activeMarketFips` and `markets.js` accepts `MARKET_STATUS` overrides; these are NOT commercial approval: a separate protected Firestore `market_admission` record and 14 evidence gates in `market-readiness.js` govern live authorization. Live protected market records were NOT accessed here; the founder reports that markets were previously closed. **Do not assume those records have reopened.** Pricing: `market-pricing.js` has an operational South Florida base of $1.00 + $0.85/mi + $0.15/min, minimum $3.00 before server-computed fees, not a validated 10%-under-current-market fare. `pricing-policy.js` defines a 90% local-market Total target and `fareauthority.js` can apply it ONLY when a published, distinct-source, condition-matched `market_reference_snapshots` record passes promotion/economic/router gates; genuine current Tri-County comparative data was NOT verified. `national-regional-pricing.js` is research-only. Refunds: founder suggests considering discontinuing discretionary refunds on the assumption that Operator money is withheld. Current `payments.js` actually **charges the Traveler on payment confirmation**, then **transfers the Operator's earned share after completed Travel**; delayed withdrawal to the Operator's bank is a separate stage. Retaining narrowly controlled corrections for non-delivery, duplicate/unauthorized charges, overcharges or other legal obligations is recommended; do NOT disable the refund machinery or eliminate Traveler dispute rights without a separately approved policy and applicable-law analysis. Sources: https://www.consumerfinance.gov/consumer-tools/credit-cards/how-to-fix-mistakes-in-your-credit-card-bill/ ; https://docs.stripe.com/connect/separate-charges-and-transfers ; https://www.nber.org/papers/w34441 . A Stripe TEST webhook callback simply means Stripe sending an authentic test payment event back to American Rider's server so both sides can be matched, without moving real money. Council standards are published decision lenses, not personal endorsements. Full concise record: [Tri-County launch and insurance/refund interpretation](docs/decisions/FOUNDER-TRICOUNTY-PRICING-REFUNDS-INSURANCE-2026-10-12.md). No live fare, refund entitlement, insurance purchase, protected market authorization or provider configuration changed.

**2026-10-12 source-led duplicate provider-event identity safety — PR UNDER REVIEW:** Re-verified live code as of `a8c7d100f13f55b8b133a7984b6a8651c1bd2d11`. Audit found `backend/providerqueue.js` accepted every existing Firestore event ID as a harmless duplicate, even when two different raw IDs normalized/truncated to the same stored key or the same ID arrived with different money data. Per [Amazon's idempotency design](https://aws.amazon.com/builders-library/making-retries-safe-with-idempotent-APIs/), repeated request ID with different intent MUST fail. Proposed narrow patch creates canonical full-event SHA-256 hash on new records and, **only on ALREADY_EXISTS**, compares stored provider/full raw ID/payload hash to protect the existing durable queue without extra first-write reads. Legacy inbox records compare against their stored payload instead; no mass rewrite. Two-pass real local Firestore emulator test of legitimate replays/colliding IDs plus signed synthetic changed-payment-amount rejection added to release gate. Source audit: [provider identity and monitoring cost](docs/audits/PROVIDER-DUPLICATE-INTENT-AND-MONITOR-COST-2026-10-12.md). **No CI/merge/deploy success claimed yet.** The minute-by-minute ALL active Travel scan remains a separate **unfixed national scaling gate**: 1,201 concurrent active Travels sustained all day imply at least 1,729,440 returned-document reads/day before other costs; 10K implies 14.4M/day, 1M implies 1.44B/day. No unsafe monitoring coverage cap adopted. No live Stripe, market, Fare or Operator insurance changes.

**2026-10-13 real Firebase password-reset quota incident — PR #158 UNDER REVIEW:** A prior GitHub live-auth check passed live account creation, lookup, token refresh, sign-in and wrong-password rejection, then FAILED with `sendOobCode: QUOTA_EXCEEDED : Exceeded daily quota for resetting passwords`. The former workflow sent a real password-reset email on every code push. Google's official [Firebase Auth quotas](https://firebase.google.com/docs/auth/limits) limit such emails. This PR retains real signup/session tests on each PR/push but requests a real reset only in the daily scheduled check or explicit manual action, and adds a twice-run offline regression for zero ordinary-CI reset emails, single planned reset, quota failure reporting and account cleanup. Daily real reset acceptance and mailbox delivery remain unverified after exhaustion; a passing PR does NOT certify the reset provider. No production auth settings or customer accounts altered by these source changes.

**2026-10-12 source-led Expo safety receipt verification — PR UNDER REVIEW:** Expo documentation independently confirms an accepted push ticket is not delivery to Apple/Google and receipt success is not handset observation. Audit found `backend/push.js` accepted safety push tickets but never fetched Expo's later provider receipts. Proposed `backend/pushreceipts.js`: durable SHA-keyed `check_in` ticket inbox (only token hash, never raw push token, GPS or event body), first receipt check at 15 minutes, bounded batch up to 100 due every 5 minutes in the existing shared leased scheduler, bounded 7-day expiry cleanup, idempotent missing/outage backoff, 24-hour no-receipt marked unverified, provider rejection recorded, transactionally safe stale-token removal, and privacy-safe workload counters. Adding the job to the **actual** `critical-sweep-recovery.js` registry as optional during quota pressure, preserving safety and refund priority. New `backend/pushreceipts.test.js` simulates 147-ticket backlog, network 503, no/invalid receipt, rotated tokens, duplicate tickets, failed database write, scheduled cleanup and no real phone/provider. Grounded in [Expo](https://docs.expo.dev/push-notifications/sending-notifications/), [Google Firestore](https://firebase.google.com/docs/firestore/best-practices), [Amazon idempotency](https://aws.amazon.com/builders-library/making-retries-safe-with-idempotent-APIs/), [NASA SWE-055](https://swehb.nasa.gov/spaces/SWEHBVD/pages/102695440/SWE-055%2B-%2BRequirements%2BValidation), [Apple notifications](https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/managing-notifications), Google SRE, OWASP MASVS, HBS service design and the **reference** Council (not actual consultation). See [evidence audit](docs/audits/PUSH-RECEIPTS-SOURCE-LED-2026-10-12.md). **Not yet verified by CI, merged or deployed.** A receipt means APNs/FCM accepted, NOT on-phone display, a 911 response or staffing. No live Fare, funds, insurance, market admissions or real passengers touched.

**2026-10-12 nationwide economic-geography source integrator — PR UNDER REVIEW (NOT FARE LIVE):** Following the founder's instruction to complete the screenshot's nationally reusable, inexpensive regional pricing design, the existing research-only `backend/national-pricing-candidate.js` is now linked through a *separate research adapter* to four dated, checksum-pinned **supplied** publisher datasets: Census 2025 county Gazetteer, Census 2023 metro/micro List 1, independently distinct BLS May 2025 OEWS area/county definitions, and BLS May 2025 occupation wages. The `scripts/assemble-national-economic-geography.py` importer preserves five-character FIPS and explicit nonmetro areas, detects missing/suppressed wages instead of guessing, requires full 50-state+DC roster, emits `bookable:false`, and never writes Firestore. The `backend/national-regional-pricing.js` bridge uses the real existing cost-floor candidate formula with an explicitly dated research cost policy (not a real Operator pay claim) and `research-hold` for unverified inputs. Synthetic tests cover 3,111 fictional counties from 51 jurisdictions **twice**, sources tampering and the 99%-to-Operator economic floor. **The genuine national publisher files have not yet been ingested, so the actual national profiles and 10%-below-market evidence are NOT certified; prior BLS archive retrieval hit HTTP 403.** Existing live fares, insurance model, jurisdictions, purchases and paid operations are unchanged. See [federal-source ingestion/limitations](docs/architecture/NATIONAL-PRICING-OFFICIAL-GEOGRAPHY-2026-10-12.md). Do not merge or claim success before all exact-head tests pass.

**2026-10-12 founder clarification — insurance lapse responsibility and operating lockout (recorded, not legally certified):** Founder directs that Operators finance their own qualifying commercial/TNC coverage and that an independently confirmed lapse, cancellation, deficiency or expiry immediately makes an Operator ineligible to commence duty, receive/accept new Travel or continue new platform operations. The founder considers an incident after a properly enforced disconnection to be outside American Rider's arranged Travel, and considers mid-Travel expiry unlikely because start-of-Travel checks prevent known expired policies. **Record these as the founder's operating intent and risk assessment, not proven facts or a waiver of claims:** offline/delayed carrier events, retroactive cancellation, exclusions, loss of status during an active prearranged Travel, and mistaken eligibility remain adversarial tests. Preserve active-Traveler safety/incident assistance and evidence after any mid-Travel adverse event; do not abandon an occupied vehicle by silently treating the Travel as private. **Florida 2026 §627.748(7)(d) still separately says TNC-maintained insurance must furnish first-dollar coverage and a defense if the Operator's coverage lapses or is insufficient.** No legal advice or synthetic test substitutes for a written qualified Florida opinion and insurer-backed lawful resolution; `company_insurance_bound` stays unsatisfied and paid-market authority unchanged. Tests/evidence matrix: [founder lapse and launch verification review](docs/audits/FOUNDER-LAPSE-AND-LAUNCH-GATES-2026-10-12.md); issue [#51](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/51). Neither real underwriting nor paid operations were authorized.

**2026-10-12 Google/Amazon/NASA plus business-school/design cross-check — PR #151 MERGED AND LIVE:** Informed by official Google Firestore index/cursor and billed-read guidance, AWS Builders’ Library bounded/idempotent retries, NASA intended-environment validation, Stripe separate charges and transfers, MIT Sloan/HBS/Stanford platform economics and Stanford d.school/IDEO/NNG/Apple human-centered guidance (see [whole-platform institutional audit](docs/audits/INSTITUTIONAL-COUNCIL-RECOVERY-2026-10-12.md)). Existing code has a concrete starvation risk: `backend/bookingrecovery.js` queried the same first 100 `awaiting_payment`, `awaiting_assignment` and cancelled/refund-pending records each 60-second sweep; a permanently unprocessed first page could indefinitely conceal later owed refunds. [PR #151](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/pull/151) now adds one bounded persisted `__name__` cursor per recovery category in one checkpoint record, retains 100-read page caps, wraps once, commits checkpoints after processing and reports quota/checkpoint failures explicitly; tests repeat 251 mixed records, independent sweeps, failures and refund idempotency. **The actual Firestore emulator verified 251 records, checkpoint persistence and query syntax; actual production indexes, billed reads and large-scale throughput remain unverified.** Founder confirms American Rider currently has **no insurance and provides none**; Operators obtain and pay for their own coverage, as ADR 0002 already states. Florida statutory contingency and `company_insurance_bound` remain unresolved, not waived. No live payment/insurer purchases, fare or markets changed. PR #148 is a stale unmergeable documentation branch, **superseded by this canonical release-history reconciliation**; do not bypass merge protections.

New audit branch uses the existing support-case idempotency capability for route-monitor safety cases, adds Resend 24h Idempotency-Key headers for alerts/location follow-ups, tests simulated failure/replay and creates a zero-provider-call forecast for 10k/100k/1m **registered** users vs active Travels. Per [external evidence audit](docs/audits/EXTERNAL-PROVIDER-AND-CAPACITY-EVIDENCE-2026-10-12.md), no physical device, actual insurer, airport, BLS dataset, signed live provider delivery, national current price or 10k real concurrent capacity has been certified. All six exact-head GitHub workflows passed, including the two full release tests. [PR #139](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/pull/139) was merged; Render backend, main website and preview independently verified LIVE at `f34c9cad` on 2026-10-12 at 06:57 UTC. This does not certify insurer obligations, staffed emergency response, individual push delivery, live payment/refund reconciliation, peak Firestore capacity, or nationwide market fares. No fares, new regions, or operating permissions were changed.

**2026-10-12 critical route-safety escalation order fix — PR #136 MERGED AND LIVE:** Inspection of `backend/monitor.js` showed that neighboring stopped vehicles triggered the congestion shortcut *before* an explicit Traveler `help` answer or Operator report of injury was examined; resumed motion could clear a previously raised emergency. Merged [PR #136](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/pull/136) gives urgent requests precedence over traffic and the motion timer, preserves emergency/escalated state, attempts both emergency pushes independently, and exposes ticket/state/push failures as degraded rather than silent success. Simulations test nearby congestion, warning replies, renewed movement, missing case and failed push. The live code does **not** guarantee actual push delivery, human intervention, 911 calling or real-world safety continuity. Synthetic tests also cover missing and stale GPS, rejected Firestore emergency writes, and 10,000 simultaneous simulated active Travels (not a live load benchmark). See [Oct 12 adversarial matrix](docs/audits/INSTITUTIONAL-ADVERSARIAL-VERIFICATION-2026-10-12.md). All six exact-head workflows passed, and Render independently confirmed backend, main web and preview live at `bd9333ab` on 2026-10-12 06:25 UTC. All remaining live-scale, provider, legal and device acceptance gates stay open.

Every research output is explicitly non-bookable, not 10%-below-market verified. Actual BLS area wages (publisher GitHub 403), operator net/cost measurements and independent current comparable quotations remain incomplete.

**2026-10-12 high-priority database outage reliability fix — MERGED AND BACKEND LIVE (PR #130):** Review of current backend revealed `runAllSweeps()` omitted returning its results; the outer scheduler then accessed `Object.values(undefined)` and could fail after executing work. A second issue: a single five-to-ten-minute quota backoff stopped all periodic safety monitoring and monetary recovery even after Firestore might recover. In [critical recovery branch](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/tree/fix/critical-sweep-quota-recovery-20261012), a new pure orchestration helper returns structured results, runs safety/insurance/dispatch/refund/settlement/provider work with independent exception handling, while deferring optional market research, account-fee maintenance, fleet work and account deletion under quota backoff; only bounded one-per-minute Firestore lease recovery checks are permitted and a truly exhausted DB runs no work. Tests inject failed lease, healthy recovery, one critical job failure, 10K repeated triggers and 10K synthetic critical batches. Existing Firestore cross-instance lease and booking/payment idempotency remain unchanged. This is not a promise of actual safe operations during a database outage or a new guarantee of under-one-minute recovery: stale leases may need up to five minutes. See [ADR 0010](docs/decisions/0010-critical-quota-sweep-recovery.md) and [issue #128](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/128). All required PR checks passed; backend deployment verified on Render at `a7f490fa`. Measured live-scale failure, lease and recovery tests remain outstanding. No live prices, payments, operators or market activation changed by this draft.

This is the **one starting index** for persistent product decisions, verified implementation, unresolved evidence and the next permissible actions. It supersedes the *status summaries* in `CURRENT_HANDOFF.md`, `docs/HANDOFF.md`, `docs/LAUNCH-NOW.md`, and older checklists. Those remain historical records, not current launch certification. The code is authority for behavior; protected Firestore/Stripe/provider records are authority for live business state; official law and qualified advice govern law; an exact GitHub workflow/deploy record is authority for build/release status. **Never infer production evidence from a sentence in this file.**

**2026-10-11 founder clarification — FREE TIERS ARE NOT A PRODUCTION STRATEGY; LEDGER IS THE HANDOFF AUTHORITY:** The previous Chicago collector cost amplifier is **fixed**, not a question to rediscover in each conversation. Every architecture review must model per-Travel contribution after ALL applicable variable/operational costs, actual operator-hours and peak simultaneous load, as distinct from raw users, synthetic loops or free-plan capacity. **2026-10-11 completed full-cost coverage audit — MODEL POSITIVE, ACTUAL FULLY LOADED PROFIT UNVERIFIED:** Earlier `docs/COST-MODEL-2026-09-29.md` already identified infrastructure and corporate cost categories, but its historical $2.00 fee examples were stale; current `backend/economics.js` sets $2.50 minimum and uses **$0.25 risk reserve + $0.25 operating allowance as internal provisions, not documented vendor invoices**. The $20 example ($17.50 Fare+$2.50 Fee) yields $17.33 Operator, $2.67 platform gross and **$1.14 domestic / $0.84 international modeled contribution**, **NOT audited net income**. Current `backend/operatorfees.js` waives the $2 Connect monthly active-account recovery at >=20 Travels: at 20, this is **10c/Travel of platform expense** not directly charged in the live quote model (it may be funded by the 25c operating allowance; do not double count). Stripe's 25c fixed charge is per payout, whereas `economics.js` assumes 6c/Travel; real payout cadence and unsuccessful Operator recovery matter. The full-cost sensitivity test **replaces the 6c+25c assumptions** with actual-style amounts rather than subtracting the same expenses twice. At 20 Travels/Operator/month, four standard payouts, waiver of the $2 account fee, no other monthly fixed expenses: domestic risk-reserved contribution is $1.05 with 25c actual other overhead per Travel, $0.70 with 60c overhead, **−$0.20** with $1.50 overhead. These are scenarios, **not actual invoices or forecasts**. One known Render Ohio starter backend is $7/mo list rate, two web/preview static sites; **actual Resend read-only usage reports 3/100 emails/day and 4/3,000/month**, matching provider Free-plan limits, so production volume requires budgeted paid email capacity rather than an assumed unlimited free service. Planned self-hosted OSRM/OTP, Firebase Blaze, HERE toll calls, SMS, **conditional Claude Sonnet 5 API calls in `backend/support.js` and `backend/operatorsupport.js`**, human case escalation, verification review and insurance/legal/CPA/support need measured spend; local document OCR is not the same as external model inference. No proof the $0.25 allowance covers them. Corporate insurer contingencies may not be assumed zero. Do not quote 'net profit' for a modeled contribution or re-open settled pricing architecture. See [fully loaded cost audit](docs/FULL-COST-COVERAGE-AUDIT-2026-10-11.md), [September inventory](docs/COST-MODEL-2026-09-29.md), `backend/fully-loaded-coverage.js` and `backend/fully-loaded-coverage.test.js`. The current PR is management-only: no fares, purchases, policy or market admission changed.

**2026-10-11 academic/institutional TNC pricing and Blaze economics — FOUNDER RESEARCH DECISION:** The founder requires published recommendations from business universities and operating platforms, not ungrounded personal advice. Independently reviewed Stanford GSB *Spatial Pricing in Ridesharing Networks* (heterogeneous geographic demand/compensation), MIT Sloan Freund/van Ryzin *Pricing Fast and Slow* (very reactive fare changes can create inefficient oscillations), Harvard Business School's Frei *Service Excellence by Design* and platform pricing coursework (service funding, traffic and rider/operator balance), and actual large-TNC streaming/simulation/controlled pilot engineering. Supported interpretation: one national server economics engine, **locally calibrated** metro/market coefficient records; slow published historical evidence refreshed by changed source/version/audits, distinct from faster own local supply/demand/ETA/traffic signals, with holdout, trust, Operator income and economic promotion gates. No blanket recommendation to scrape other platforms or to run surge pricing. **Existing fl-southeast price is a route-variable Fare using $1 base + $0.85/mile + $0.15/minute ($3 minimum), with SHARED REGION COEFFICIENTS**, not a fixed Traveler Total; evidence as of Sep 20 and no commissioned Florida live independent quote feed. New market without an explicit record still fails closed. Synthetic economics under current `backend/economics.js` at $17.50 Fare + $2.50 Fee/one $20 domestic Traveler Total yields $17.33 Operator and **$1.14 modeled platform contribution** after $0.25 contingency and $0.25 operating allowance, versus $0.75 minimum; international card contribution **$0.84**, only $0.09 margin above the modeled minimum. This is NOT full-company profit until legal/insurance, infrastructure, provider and real labor costs are measured. Conditional Blaze example at us-central1 Standard document rate, 100 billed reads + 40 writes per Travel: 10K daily Travels roughly **$18.81/30-day**, 100K/day **$197.01/30-day**, 1M/day **$1,979.01/30-day** for those document operations only, assuming same daily free allowances and constant load. No billing tier checked/changed; no one-million concurrent users or nationwide cash economics certified. See [ADR 0005](docs/decisions/0005-institutional-pricing-blaze-and-florida-assessment.md) and `backend/paid-scale-economics.test.js`.

**2026-10-11 founder insurance-compliance conclusion — DOCUMENTED, NOT CERTIFIED BY OWNER APPROVAL:** Founder explicitly concludes the American Rider Operator-funded, independently verified insurance + no-duty/no-acceptance on lapse fully satisfies Florida insurance law, particularly for unrelated post-logoff personal accidents; he considers failures during an insured Travel exceptional. Preserve that interpretation and D02/ADR 0002 business policy. Official 2026 §627.748(7)(b)-(c) permits Operator insurance for logged-on/prearranged Travel; (7)(d) still requires TNC-maintained coverage to pay first-dollar and defend when coverage proves lapsed/insufficient **during the relevant covered period**. A software fail-closed check cannot retroactively cure invalid insurance when a claim already arose, nor does founder's risk assessment waive statutory text. **Do not mark actual insurer/company-contingency evidence compliant without independent facts; do not fabricate purchase or bypass market gate.** A legal-opinion letter is not a government filing/permit. The distinction and founder's explicit dissent are recorded in ADR 0005. No changes to live insurance or admission gates.

**2026-10-11 national market-pricing doctrine — SOURCE-RELEASE DRIVEN, NOT 1000 CITIES HOURLY:** PRs #101/#103 already disabled the hourly uncommissioned Chicago import and implemented no-source zero-Firestore work, bounded records, append-only evidence and deduplicated snapshots. These are NATIONAL capacity protections, not just Chicago fixes. The prior public Chicago fetch was retrospective fare-distribution research, not live pricing; its city records are published roughly monthly with revisions and rounded fares. NYC TLC trips also publish monthly with lag. Current Florida county source plans exist but NO licensed/live independent quote collectors are connected. The South Florida production rate curve remains static with dated evidence; a declared `externalObservationMinutes=60` freshness target does NOT mean actual live competitor price data exists. For new markets, use issuer release/version signals and local representative audits, source-specific cadence and two independent families, strict rights, scoped market-level promotion, and our real own marketplace/route signals for operational cost decisions. Avoid hour-by-hour historical scraping and needless per-city database work. This PR adds ordering-insensitive evidence fingerprinting and 1,000-idle-market / source-change tests; see [pricing evidence design](docs/architecture/NATIONAL-PRICING-EVIDENCE-CADENCE.md). Promotion to production still requires six passing exact-head workflows and independently verified Render deployment; this documentation is not live data source activation, fare promotion or paid-market admission.

**2026-10-11 private Founder Command exists — AUTHENTICATED TOOL NOT CONNECTED HERE:** The Plugin Creator lists the private `american-rider-founder-command` v0.1.0, `american-rider-operations` v0.1.0, and private founder control app v1.0.0; the first plugin's actual MCP manifest points to `https://american-rider-server-3d7x.onrender.com/mcp` with OAuth scopes market read/plan/activate/pause. The production server source already implements market_status/prepare_market/open_region/pause_market. **Do not rebuild them or create another plugin.** The current chat still does not expose a callable authenticated market tool. **2026-10-11 founder's off-duty insurance interpretation — RECORDED, NOT A STATUTORY WAIVER:** The founder states that an incident after an Operator has genuinely ended operations with American Rider is private/off-duty and outside the TNC platform journey. Florida §627.748 defines the relevant TNC insured periods as logged onto the network and engaged in a prearranged ride; subsection (7)(d) addresses insufficient/lapsed coverage in those applicable periods. Independently enforced off-duty lockout is therefore materially relevant. Do NOT infer that turning an Operator off duty retroactively terminates an accepted ride or an accident/claim that arose while logged on or riding; D02 remains company-policy settled and the distinct company-insurance contingency remains fact-dependent/unverified. No insurance policy, legal status, evidence or market gate was altered.

**2026-10-11 verified webhook failure and PR #114 DEPLOYED — FIRESTORE RECOVERY NOT CERTIFIED:** Three authorized Resend synthetic messages succeeded at provider level (delivered, bounced, complained), but signed callback attempts originally timed out; the existing backend logged Firestore quota failures. PR #114 merged as `1c117186f13e1d3b85490ab0fcda4b6bb7d802ce`, passed six exact-head workflows and was verified live on all three Render services. Its four-second durable-enqueue deadline returned a real retryable **HTTP 503** after replay of a previously signed synthetic event at 06:37 UTC, with Render log `[resend] durable webhook receipt refused, reason=write_deadline, elapsedMs=4000`. **The signed endpoint was reached, but Firestore did not confirm storage**; no 2xx/processed event is proved. Do not treat this as uncommissioned *code*, nor as fully commissioned *provider delivery*; these are different states. Never weaken signed provider, insurance or payment gates; follow [issue #85](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/85) and [issue #102](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/102). No billing plan was modified.

**2026-10-11 Resend early-callback correlation race — PR #112 DEPLOYED:** On review of the actual newly merged delivery event handler, a signed provider event that arrives before the outbound request saves `providerMessageId` returned `{ok:true,unmatched:true}`, causing the durable queue to mark it irrecoverably complete. This is a possible asynchronous timing race; no production incident has been observed. Following [Resend's webhook recovery guidance](https://resend.com/changelog/headless-webhook-api) and the existing bounded `providerqueue.js` retry/dead-letter mechanism, a missing exact message match now returns `ok:false` so the queued verified event retries after correlation persists. The wrong-recipient case remains ignored, unrelated mail remains filtered, and a delivery event never approves insurance. Added a deterministic event-before-outbound-acknowledgment → retry-after-save test. A second related failure window exists if the request's message ID is saved before the Operator's current request stamp; reverse those two successful-send writes and pin ordering in a regression test so a fast bounce can create the correct self-service contact issue. PR #112 passed all six exact-head release workflows, merged and was independently verified live in backend, main web and preview. It is not a real successful 2xx provider callback or insurer verification.

**2026-10-11 Resend insurance delivery-feedback repair — PR #111 DEPLOYED; LIVE CALLBACK STORAGE UNVERIFIED:** Per [issue #85](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/85), production insurance confirmation requests already store the unique Resend message ID in `insurance_status_requests`, but connected Resend showed **zero webhook subscriptions**. Resend's official webhook contract differentiates `email.sent`, `email.delivered`, permanent bounce, failure, complaint and suppression. Source: https://resend.com/docs/webhooks/event-types ; https://resend.com/docs/webhooks/verify-webhooks-requests ; Svix manual signature/HMAC specification https://docs.svix.com/receiving/verifying-payloads/how-manual ; Google SRE launch failure testing https://sre.google/sre-book/testing-reliability/ . Candidate uses Node built-in crypto for exact raw-body Svix HMAC SHA256 verification with fixed clock skew, bounded payload and constant-time compare, and reuses the existing durable `provider_events` inbox. Only messages issued from American Rider's insurance mailbox enter this queue; matching by provider message ID and intended recipient ensures a receipt cannot change insurance requests. Out-of-order and replay events cannot downgrade delivery evidence. Permanent delivery failure creates a *self-service contact issue* only for the latest unconfirmed request; it never changes `lastVerifiedAt`, insurance status, policy coverage or eligibility. No new vendor, package, automated bulk broker mail, account spending, or actual status confirmation. PR #111 passed six exact-head workflows and was deployed on all three Render services. One enabled Resend webhook was registered; its dedicated signing secret was synchronized privately into Render. Real Resend synthetic messages generated signed callbacks; HTTP 503 after PR #114 established an actual Firestore persistence blocker, not completed event processing. Only authenticated, independent insurer/broker status verification can establish a live policy. No market authorization follows from email transport.


**2026-10-11 Teen pickup timestamp bypass — PR #110 DEPLOYED:** The real `backend/teenpickup.js` PIN provisioner stores `verifiedAt:null` until an authenticated PIN verification sets a server-side numeric timestamp. The existing `backend/travelprogress.js` onboard check used `Number.isFinite(Number(verifiedAt))`; in JavaScript `Number(null) === 0`, so the *actual unverified state* could pass the boarding gate. The repo tests only checked a missing timestamp `{}`, not `null`. Following OWASP object/action-level authorization and Google SRE negative-path testing, the candidate repair accepts only a positive, finite **safe integer** timestamp no later than the server transition time, with matching `teenMayBoard` presentation helper and regression coverage for `null`, zero, false, empty/string/future/malformed data. The continuous synthetic Teen journey now uses the real `verifiedAt:null` provisioning shape. The fix passed all six exact-head CI workflows, merged as `86a0dd813da1967dd1260a2c59c3bcf6b1956545` and was independently confirmed on all three Render services. This does **not** certify a real device/PIN pilot. No new service/cost, relaxed PIN policy, real Teen passenger, production enrollment or admission writes.


**2026-10-11 national architecture review — PR #103 merged and deployed:** The Founder directed that consequential engineering choices begin with authoritative current web research and review of exact production code; avoid assumptions and repeated conversational churn. See [National operating model](docs/architecture/NATIONAL-OPERATING-MODEL.md) and [issue #102](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/102). Decision: city catalog membership never authorizes background scraping/import; permanent Travel/payment/compliance audit is distinct from ephemeral real-time positions; urgent safety/payment/recovery remains watchdog-protected, while research and routine maintenance are change/due-driven. No additional vendor purchases or market authorizations were approved. PR #103 makes configured markets with no live authorized collectors a **zero-Firestore-work** case; hashes source batches to skip repeated data, stores historical observations only on create, and avoids snapshot rewrites absent new observations. Six exact-head CI workflows passed and backend/web/preview Render deployments matched the merge SHA. Live Firestore usage and per-Travel cost are NOT yet certified. Chat context-window capacity cannot be read as a reliable percent; use this ledger and exact code to hand off.
 

**2026-10-11 proportional-scale remediation deployed (PR #105):** Founder reaffirmed official external engineering sources BEFORE significant implementation, complete Council acceptance, and an economic, automated national operating system. Code audit found every `/operator/online` location heartbeat directly requests Stripe Connect account status, while authenticated `account.updated` webhook handling and live `/travel/accept` provider rechecks are already built. Research: Stripe Connect webhooks/rate limits, Firebase Firestore read/write scaling and pricing, Google SRE capacity planning, Render auto-scaling. The deployed targeted change caches only recent server-bound positive Stripe attestations, expires them at 24 hours, preserves signed webhook revocation + acceptance-time direct status, and transactionally rejects a raced downgrade. All six exact-head checks passed and Render backend/web/preview deployments were independently verified. See [architectural research and safety boundaries](docs/architecture/OPERATOR-PRESENCE-AND-STRIPE-ELIGIBILITY.md), [holistic launch #104](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/104), [capacity #94](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/94) and [usage #102](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/102). **Do not equate this with automatic million-Operator capacity, verified Firebase billing or commercial launch.** No new paid services, admission or insurance/screening decisions are authorized by this patch.

**2026-10-11 active-Operator background renewal defect — DEPLOYED as PR #106:** Inspected actual `src/backend/presence.ts` and `backend/server.js`: native background task submits only lat/lng, while `/operator/online` demanded `b.insuranceExpiry`, refused renewal and offlined the fleet record. Per Expo location/task documentation, a top-level native background task must not depend on React foreground state. Fix derives expiry only from accepted server-side `users/{uid}.documents.insurance` evidence (including a verified adjudicator expiry), after `assessOperator()` independently checks insurance/screening/disclosure; invalid, expired, absent, rejected records fail closed. A device can no longer extend an accepted policy by supplying a date. No new third party, billing, database collection or market permission. All six exact-head CI workflows and backend/web/preview Render deployment SHAs passed. Real phone/background tests remain unverified. Council: Ford (one shared renewal), Rockefeller (no new writes/services), Jobs/Emirates (avoid invisible loss of service), Google/Expo (server authority, actual native lifecycle). Sources: https://docs.expo.dev/versions/latest/sdk/location/ and https://firebase.google.com/docs/firestore/client/libraries .

**2026-10-11 Operator foreground recovery and geocoding review — PR #107 DEPLOYED:** The legacy Operator app uses Traveler `resolveCurrentDeparture()` for every 90-second duty renewal, including reverse geocoding, and is not designed to revalidate immediately when returning from OS background suspension. Official Expo SDK 57 explicitly warns reverse geocoding is resource-consuming, `timeInterval` is Android-only and real location callbacks are OS-controlled; Apple Core Location describes background suspension and permission constraints; React Native AppState supplies foreground recovery notification. Proposed low-cost remedy: for Operator initial duty/foreground renewals request only fresh, bounded-age/accuracy coordinates (no address lookup); recheck once on foreground resume; avoid overlapping renewals; report explicit server refusals; keep signed server qualification, 5-minute stale-presence fail-closed and market/Stripe checks unchanged. This does **NOT** guarantee stationary-iPhone background callbacks or certify a phone/device campaign. All six exact-head CI checks and all three Render service merge SHAs were verified. Signed physical iOS/Android evidence remains pending; do not claim fully resolved. Council: Ford shared duty renewal, Rockefeller remove avoidable reverse-geocode cost, Jobs/Apple reduce silent unavailable state, institutional research/Emirates require actual evidence and honest recovery. Sources: https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v57.0.0/sdk/location/ ; https://reactnative.dev/docs/appstate ; https://developer.apple.com/documentation/corelocation/handling-location-updates-in-the-background ; https://sre.google/sre-book/service-best-practices/ .

**2026-10-11 full synthetic campaign augmentation — PR #108 DEPLOYED:** Founder ordered exhaustive synthetic Traveler/Operator/insurance/screening/email/Stripe/provider failure testing based on Google SRE launch reviews; Firebase Emulator Suite isolation; Stripe payment/Connect testing; Resend provider test addresses; FTC FCRA; Expo/Apple native test limits and Florida 2026 TNC insurance statute. For proof, the prior CI campaigns run unit suites separately; they are not one complete continuous real-user journey. A new integrated in-memory test now drives the real backend `acceptOffer` and `progressTravel` across reserved simulated charge evidence, concurrent accepts, wrong-party attempts, source-refused payout state, pickup, Teen PIN, completion, one payout-due write, and three-county admission evidence omissions, with eight eligibility-failure classes. It does not claim production Stripe signatures, real CRA/insurer confirmation, Resend recipient inbox, real GPS, permissions, legal approval or real-money settlement. **Never copy synthetic proof to protected `market_admission` or unverified Operator records.** Stripe sandbox is now connected for read-only/provider-side verification: existing sandbox PaymentIntent/transfer records are not American Rider production reconciliation. Resend sending domain is verified but currently has zero delivery-event webhooks; real inbound receipt processing is not established. Live Stripe keys and the two active production webhook destinations must not be reconfigured to test a sandbox. Existing [launch audit #104](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/104) remains the single cross-functional campaign. See [research-based release test matrix](docs/verification/SYNTHETIC-END-TO-END-CAMPAIGN.md). The Open South Florida instruction remains authorized in principle only *when real gates and protected named authenticated command pass*. Florida §627.748(7)(d) insurance response remains an independent counsel/broker issue.

**2026-10-11 receipt/email adversarial test — IN PR REVIEW, NOT DEPLOYED:** Following official [OWASP HTML output encoding](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Cross_Site_Scripting_Prevention_Cheat_Sheet), reviewed real `backend/email.js` and found that receipt/payout `row(k,v)` directly interpolated route labels, Operator display names, Travel class and Travel Number as HTML. Malformed/untrusted values could inject formatting and misleading receipt content. Small patch encodes the five HTML text-node special characters at output boundary only; preserves sender, Stripe accounting, exact amounts, links and plain-text/subject values. New `backend/email-safety.test.js` runs hostile HTML and normal Unicode data against real receipt/payout templates, then *mocks* Resend HTTP 200/202/400/429/500/503/offline response behavior; real inbox receipt and provider-delivery webhook remain unverified. No Resend API call is sent from tests. Do not equate mock delivery with Resend proof. Governed by Council's trusted communications, economical testing and research-first review; require all six exact-head CI suites before any merge and independent Render SHA afterwards. Commissioning matrix remains `docs/verification/SYNTHETIC-END-TO-END-CAMPAIGN.md`; existing issue #104 tracks remaining external gates. 

## 1. Founder decisions — settled, do not relitigate

| ID | Policy / invariant | Decision record |
| --- | --- | --- |
| D01 | Founder operates American Rider using an iPhone/iPad. Operations must be exception-driven; no routine manual Operator approvals, market-by-market data entry, or million-email workflows. Economically efficient means **right-sized, reliable paid-scale service** when necessary, not dependence on free tiers; reuse proven vendors without unnecessary charges. | [ADR 0001](docs/decisions/0001-phone-first-national-operations.md); [ADR 0004](docs/decisions/0004-paid-scale-capacity-economics.md) |
| D02 | **Operator-funded insurance**: American Rider does **not** intend to purchase, provide or sell Operator automobile insurance. Each Operator supplies qualifying coverage for their chosen jurisdiction, insured person, vehicle and TNC/for-hire use. Independently confirm initial and continuing status; expired, canceled, insufficient, adverse or unverifiable coverage **fails closed**. The founder does not authorize a platform-insurance purchase or a redesign. **Separate, unresolved legal issue:** Whether this zero-platform-insurance business model satisfies the TNC's obligations under Fla. Stat. §627.748(7)(d) must be established by competent Florida legal review; code cannot adjudicate that question. Do not quietly mark it settled or assume it is waived by suspension. | [ADR 0002](docs/decisions/0002-operator-funded-insurance.md) |
| D03 | **Provider-neutral screening**, paid by the Operator wherever legally and commercially available. Verify lawful CRA reporting for American Rider's actual permissible purpose, state-specific elements, consent, report authenticity and adverse-action process. An old report/operator declaration is not a pass. Do not require Checkr or any single provider. | [ADR 0003](docs/decisions/0003-provider-neutral-screening.md) |
| D04 | One national software engine with explicit **state-specific** legal, insurance and screening policies. A plan, county boundary, state registry entry or a successful software deploy does **not** activate paid Travel. Reuse actual statewide evidence among counties only within its lawful scope; uncommissioned jurisdictions fail closed. Founder authorized planning/opening **Miami-Dade, Broward and Palm Beach when verified launch gates pass** on 2026-10-10; do not repeatedly request the same intent. Market activation still needs authenticated authority and current evidence. | [ADR 0001](docs/decisions/0001-phone-first-national-operations.md) |
| D05 | 99% of the **Travel Fare** to the Operator, with the exact server-authoritative pricing and platform fees in `backend/economics.js`; Traveler sees one total. No manual assumed flat-fee schedule or guessed competitor price. Fail closed on unknown tolls/fees. |
| D06 | **Founder-ratified Council:** strategic reference figures **Henry Ford, John D. Rockefeller and Steve Jobs**; published institutional guidance from **Harvard Business School, Stanford GSB, MIT Sloan, leading universities, Apple and respected software/design organizations**; distinguished **Emirates/first-class hospitality** as service benchmark. Apply the exact rubric **institutional, authoritative, sophisticated, elegant, seamless, trustworthy, economically disciplined and slightly futuristic**, with quiet authority and one-founder phone-first operation. These are reference standards, **not endorsements or actual advisors**. **[Canonical Council Standard](docs/COUNCIL-STANDARDS.md)** governs every technical, business, language, screen and launch decision. Never advertise fabricated evidence or success. |
| D07 | **Founder-ratified paid-scale commercial infrastructure (2026-10-11):** Plan American Rider as a commercially financed transport platform for **thousands to millions of Travelers and Operators**, including appropriately sized paid Firebase, Render and provider tiers. **Never optimize the architecture to remain on a free plan**, use free-quota exhaustion as proof a deployed repair failed, or ask the Founder to re-open a settled Chicago collector issue without new post-fix proof. Free allowances are incidental. Require *measured* per-completed-Travel contribution economics including 99% Fare entitlement, Stripe/Connect, infrastructure, Firestore/index reads, active-duty location, maps, routing, communications, trust/safety, dispute/refund risk and support; project peak loads, alerts, headroom and scaling thresholds. Prevent gratuitous writes/vendor calls regardless of price. This **policy does not itself approve a specific billing purchase, price, contract, actual million-user certification or paid market activation**. | [ADR 0004](docs/decisions/0004-paid-scale-capacity-economics.md), [national model](docs/architecture/NATIONAL-OPERATING-MODEL.md), [issue #102](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/102) |
| D08 | **Published institutional pricing guidance and conditional Blaze model (2026-10-11):** Keep national Fare/economics engine and **locally calibrated metro coefficients**; never advertise current continuously updated local independent prices unless connected evidence exists. Slow historical datasets use publication releases and independent audits, own local supply/dispatch metrics use bounded live operational triggers; constrain price movements by holdouts, actual rider/operator reliability and per-Travel economics. Synthetic Blaze document-operation projections suggest current modeled contributions could readily pay read/write charges at large Travel volumes, but real provider costs, p95/p99 throughput, carrier obligations, pay-as-you-go billing state and full company profit remain unverified. Founder considers existing Operator-funded insurance gates compliant; that is a founder assertion, not proof §627.748(7)(d) is satisfied or a statutory waiver. Avoid reopening PRs #101/#103/#116. | [ADR 0005](docs/decisions/0005-institutional-pricing-blaze-and-florida-assessment.md), [ADR 0004](docs/decisions/0004-paid-scale-capacity-economics.md) |
| D09 | **Founder-requested complete cost coverage (2026-10-11):** $1.14/$0.84 per-$20-Travel numbers are **modeled contributions after internal provisions**, not net corporate profit. All actual costs must be independently priced or explicitly marked unknown: payouts and waived $2 active Connect accounts, fleet idle hours, Firestore/index/listeners, hosting and routing/tiles/search/tolls, messages/voice, documents, support/chargebacks/insurance verification, company-level legal/CPA/insurance obligations, marketing and corporate fixed overhead. Do not count the same reserve or assumed overhead as both a provision and a vendor invoice. Test per-Travel margin, per-duty-Operator-hour cost, 0/100/1K/10K/100K/1M daily Travel loads, fixed-cost break-even, card mix, payouts and low/high/refund/dispute cases before claiming full profitability. Do not use unpriced categories as $0; model them as scenario variables, then reconcile to invoices when providers/account billing become available. | [Fully loaded audit](docs/FULL-COST-COVERAGE-AUDIT-2026-10-11.md), [original cost map](docs/COST-MODEL-2026-09-29.md), [ADR 0004](docs/decisions/0004-paid-scale-capacity-economics.md) |
| D10 | **Founder-approved fleet economics and active Connect fee policy (2026-10-11):** Estimate actual company expense and retained pre-tax margin for 50/100/200/500/1,000 Operators using demand, duty hours, daily completed Travels, actual payment/Connect fees, monthly active-account charges, payout frequency, risk reserve, paid maps fallback (MapLibre/OpenFreeMap currently; Stadia credit price as backup), self-hosted OSRM/OTP capacity, Blaze document/index/network/storage, Resend, SMS, support, monitoring, corporate/insurance/legal and staged peak capacity. A verified model has explicit assumptions and losses at low utilization; **do not assert any fixed startup overhead vanishes or that profit can never be negative**. Founder directs **REMOVE 20-Travel monthly waiver** for $2 active bank-payout Connect account cost: each active payout month has one transparent Operator-funded $2 pass-through regardless of monthly Travel count; ZERO monthly payout => ZERO charge; preserve 99% Fare and one Traveler Total. **Policy ratified, billing rollout NOT LIVE until consent:** publish clear five-language agreement/onboarding disclosure, obtain explicit fee/off-session authorization from impacted Operators, sandbox-test 0/1/19/20/100 Travels/payout/failed payment/duplicate/duty and implement bounded monthly billing without extra rides-count query before deploy. Consider 1.5% Stripe account debit only with eligibility/consent; else existing card collection and its gross-up. New finance forecast is MANAGEMENT ONLY and does not change live quote/Stripe charging. **Corrected daily-default** 50-Operator/3,000-Travel monthly scenario has **$6,369 modeled expenses, $8,070 gross receipts (including $60 recovered Operator account fees), and $1,701 pre-tax remainder** after a **provisional, not actual** $1,500/month company budget plus $3/registered Operator. The previous $6,249/$1,821 assumed four *weekly* bank payouts without configuring that schedule, and was also not optimized across published paid Stadia tiers. Bare technology-only scenarios can omit the corporate placeholder but are not certified net income; legal/insurance/permit/support obligations remain unpriced. At low utilization company may lose money. See [ADR 0006](docs/decisions/0006-fleet-forecast-and-connect-fee.md), `backend/fleet-economics-forecast.js` and test. | [ADR 0006](docs/decisions/0006-fleet-forecast-and-connect-fee.md), [Full-cost audit](docs/FULL-COST-COVERAGE-AUDIT-2026-10-11.md), [Stripe Connect pricing](https://stripe.com/connect/pricing) |

## 2. Verified software — do not build it again

**Onboarding / insurance**
- `backend/markets.js` + `backend/regions.js` determine county and jurisdiction using official Florida county geometry and a single `fl-southeast` region for Miami-Dade/Broward/Palm Beach. `backend/jurisdictions/us.json` lists 50 states, DC and five territories; identity records are not an operating license.
- `backend/insurance-jurisdictions.js` implements Florida's Operator coverage requirements, including logged-on 50/100/25 thousand, $1m during a ride and PIP. Unknown state insurance requirements are not silently copied from Florida.
- `backend/qualification.js` recomputes status on every relevant qualification/duty/acceptance path. It checks documents, insured and vehicle identity, policy period and TNC use, minimum limits, screening, suspension, payout readiness and disclosure. `backend/matching.js`/server gates also reject lapse and stale presence. No saved "approved" flag is permanent authority.
- `backend/insurance-monitoring.js`: independent *first* status confirmation (an uploaded declaration alone is not proof); 30-day refresh, bounded 5-day attestation grace, adverse events immediately reject, 250-record rotating cursor. `backend/insurance-contact-trust.js` + `backend/insurance-official-source-router.js` check provider contact identity; Florida DFS business-license email exact match is **contact evidence only**, not proof of a live policy. `backend/insurance-reminders.test.js` covers bounded notification retries; PR #84 deployed.
- Real million-Operator-throughput and universal insurer status feeds are **NOT** commissioned. A 250-per-minute sweep would take at least 4,000 minutes for 1 million due records, excluding real latency. Prefer verified carrier batches/signed event feeds *only when real providers support them*; use low-volume verified email as fallback, not a nationwide million-email plan.

**Screening / regional scale**
- `backend/screening-jurisdictions.js` contains a Florida-specific three-year rule. `backend/screening.js` handles assessment/expiry/warning, `backend/external-screening.js` and `backend/screening-handoff.js` implement provider-neutral case/consent and verified-review pathways; `backend/qualification.js` blocks incompatible old-state screening.
- The current screening case/handoff design uses **named Operations review** for authenticated external CRA evidence, not an already commissioned direct signed CRA feed. `SCREENING_EVIDENCE_WORKFLOW_VERIFIED` is a production sign-off flag to set *after* the external pilot, not a bypass.
- **2026-10-10 verified deployed repair:** PR #90 merged at `24c0fbc4785f7644ae1e2e8055e1b750c8d0a828`; all six exact-head CI workflows passed and Render backend/main web/preview each reported this exact merge as **live** on 2026-10-10. `backend/screening.js` has a persisted indexed screening-renewal cursor with capped scan pages/end-of-range wrap, renewal-cycle markers and idempotent durable expiry cases; [Issue #88](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/88) is closed. `backend/screening-pagination.test.js` tests 620 synthetic Operators, multiple pages, renewal, expiry blocks and checkpoint failure. **This does not certify one-million-Operator real throughput, CRA commissioning, push delivery or commercial eligibility.** Screening expiry independently blocks qualification.

**Markets / finances / administration**
- `backend/market-readiness.js` is the sole `market_admission` authority: independently reviewed, unexpired, manifest-version-bound evidence in 14 domains, dependencies, transactions and audit log. `backend/server.js` restricts activation to named authenticated Operations/MFA and production/live conditions. **2026-10-10 change in review (not yet deployed):** one-action `Open South Florida` within `/ops/markets` and generic authenticated `/ops/markets/regions/:regionId/activate` call `activateRegion()` to *atomically* recheck and open all configured counties together, or group real missing evidence in one explanation without opening any. Does not bypass gates, invent documentation, provision a CRA or allow arbitrary unauthenticated chat activation. Verify merge SHA and production before changing this to live. Every paid new booking independently rechecks active admission; `backend/market-expansion.js` only stores *plans*. Do not set `MARKET_STATUS` or Firestore documents directly to circumvent it.
- `backend/server.js` payment/dispatch and `backend/economics.js` own price and money authority; the app must not authorise a fare, transfer, settlement, market or screening pass.
- PR #87 fixed repeated scheduler sweeps with cross-instance lease and same-instance single-flight; all six exact-head CI workflows passed and it merged as `2c52761597f90fc2a16d7dea01095e235c4afdeb`. Render backend/main web/preview were checked live on that SHA on 2026-10-10. Re-verify deployment after later merges.

**2026-10-10 ChatGPT connector (PR under review; not deployed or connected):** The founder has authorized adding secure ChatGPT market commands. `backend/chatgpt-mcp.js` implements a custom MCP endpoint on the existing Render backend with OAuth 2.1 authorization code + S256 PKCE, existing named Ops MFA sign-in/consent, credential-rotation revocation, hashed Firestore access/refresh credentials, restricted scopes, and four explicit tools: read market readiness, record an expansion proposal, conditionally activate a configured region, and pause a county with fleet shutdown. It reuses `market-readiness.js`; delegated audit method distinguishes ChatGPT actions from direct Ops UI. Tool publishing, user OAuth consent and actual protected commercial opening remain NOT performed. Verify protocol/interoperability, client-ID metadata and all release gates before merging or connecting; never expose plaintext credentials.

**2026-10-10 production Firestore incident — mitigation pending PR #98:** Render at 2026-10-11 00:55:58 UTC recorded `8 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED: Quota exceeded` with a 60-second scheduler lease-renewal timeout; the independent 01:01:53 UTC named Ops sign-in log recorded `authenticator_code_rejected`. The live service stayed online, but quota and OAuth commissioning are not recovered/confirmed. Thirteen recurring sweeps execute on a one-minute baseline. PR #98 reduces only nonurgent maintenance cadence (30m screening and insurance, 60m Family age-out/account deletion and market-reference, 6h monthly fees); dispatch, live safety, provider-event handling, payment settlement, pending market fleet cleanup remain each minute. Explicit quota exhaustion triggers bounded in-memory retry delay capped at 10m; live admission/eligibility gates are unchanged. **Root cause and Firebase account billing tier require independent Firebase usage evidence, which is not accessible through GitHub/Render.** Check exact PR CI, merge SHA, live Render and production Firestore usage before classifying the incident resolved. No market activated.

**2026-10-10 founder sign-in latency repair (in review, NOT live):** Verified Render production evidence includes `ops: mfa_store_error` at 2026-10-11T01:29Z and earlier genuine MFA-session successes at 01:05Z and 01:06Z. Do not attribute every multi-minute sign-in delay to an expired authenticator code: TOTP validation occurs before the Firestore anti-replay transaction. `backend/opslatency.js` adds bounded 8-second waits for the MFA replay transaction and ten individual Operations dashboard queries; deferred results do not issue sessions, board failures remain visibly unavailable, and no OTP window is widened or security gate bypassed. These timeouts improve responsiveness, **not database capacity/quota**, and cannot certify successful OAuth or commercial admission until tested against live Firebase.

**2026-10-10 Chicago background import incident — ROOT CAUSE IDENTIFIED, REPAIRS #101 AND #103 DEPLOYED:** An hourly research-only City of Chicago pricing-data collector fetched up to 1,000 records and wrote each observation into `market_reference_observations`; repeated runs could generate about **24,000 Firestore writes/day** even though Chicago was not an admitted operating market. Historical Firebase aggregate screenshots showed ~37K reads/~24K writes (later ~35K/~21K); these totals alone cannot allocate each billed write to a source. **PR #101** (merge `c922ae5fa089741bc260df710a2f75bced802af4`) disabled uncommissioned research-only city ingestion. **PR #103** (merge `c28b24664df50506ef93f3c420aa5dda75ffc304`) added zero-Firestore work when no authorized collector, unchanged batch hashing, append-only/deduplicated observations and no needless reference snapshots; both deployed and have source regression coverage in `backend/market-reference-cost.test.js`. **DO NOT re-open this as an unknown defect** without measured post-fix collection-level evidence tied to an exact later SHA. The separate incident of Firestore quota exhaustion and webhook enqueue 503 does not reverse these verified code repairs; for any residual use, measure *new* collection-level billed operations. See [issue #102](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/102) and [ADR 0004](docs/decisions/0004-paid-scale-capacity-economics.md). Free allowances are not a commercial capacity plan; normal paid usage plus measured scalable architecture is the founder's standing decision.

## 3. Real-world commissioning — explicitly NOT established by code

| Gate | 2026-10-10 evidence classification | Exact next proof required |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Florida zero-platform-insurance legal interpretation | **UNRESOLVED**; Founder policy settled, regulatory sufficiency not determined | Florida-qualified legal opinion addressing §627.748(7)(d), duty to defend, lapse-between-checks, and relevant TNC obligations. Do not make a purchase or change D02 without new founder authorization. |
| First real insurance status source | **NOT VERIFIED as completed** | Authenticated licensed insurer/broker confirms exact Operator/vehicle/policy using an actual supported channel; prove adverse and unchanged-policy cases, genuine delivery and safe exception fallback. Contact licensing data is insufficient. |
| Approved CRA screening and automation pilot | **NOT COMMISSIONED** per `docs/SCREENING-PROVIDER-COMMISSIONING.md` | Real CRA permission, contract/allowed purpose, Operator authorization, Florida elements and secure source-authenticated report receipt; prove pass/hold/adverse and device duty lockout; determine directly funded/Operator-funded model from provider terms. No real provider feed is presumed. |
| Production capacity and profitable scale | **PAID-SCALE POLICY RATIFIED; LIVE BILLING/MEASURED CAPACITY NOT ESTABLISHED HERE** | Plan normal paid Firebase/Render/service tiers and verify collection-level billed reads/writes, peak operator location fanout, infrastructure/Stripe/provider costs, reserve/risk and **per-completed-Travel contribution**. Test staged volume, concurrency and recovery; free-tier limits are NOT a platform design goal. A historical Spark quota screenshot is not a fresh root-cause diagnosis. See D07, ADR 0004 and issue #102. |
| Paid Travel and money | **NO controlled production reconciliation evidence recorded in the audited repository** | One authorized low-value end-to-end charge, transfer, toll, refund/dispute and ledger reconciliation with Stripe actual IDs, fees and retries. |
| Mobile and safety/release | **NO signed exact-SHA physical-device campaign evidence recorded** | Signed iOS and Android tests including background presence, push, auth, booking, dispatch, recovery, emergency, Family/Teen, Smart Travel, accessibility, all buttons and navigation; record exact build and observed results. |
| Jurisdiction/facilities and company obligations | **NOT fully attested** | Florida TNC law, biennial CPA, airport/port or restricted-area permissions, safety/accessible service, real operator supply and operational response evidence as applicable; record reviewed references. |
| Commercial tri-county activation | **NOT CONFIRMED ACTIVE** | Authenticate to protected `/ops/markets` or delegated least-privilege interface, read live `market_admission` records, verify each county, authorize via existing engine only when actual evidence passes. No connected GitHub/Render action has authenticated to protected Ops. |

**If proof is absent, say "unverified" rather than "failed" or "complete".** Present an exact county's actual status only after reading the protected live record. CI, deployment and synthetic stress tests are not production provider, capacity, legal or customer evidence.

Three nonbinding market research emails (two insurance specialists and one CRA) were sent from American Rider Communications on 2026-10-10; no policy was purchased or report ordered. Founder subsequently reaffirmed no platform-insurance purchases. Do not send a new purchase request without explicit permission.
2. **Complete and independently test secure founder ChatGPT connection** under [issue #86](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/86): exact CI, OAuth client compatibility, revocation, production deployment, then founder-installed connector and real protected market status. **Do not treat a plugin skill alone as an authenticated Ops connection.**
3. **Finish scale and service defects**: [#88 screening pagination (deployed, closed)](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/88), [#85 Resend delivery events](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/85) and [#86 authenticated chat-first market commands](https://github.com/chadkhalil21-svg/American-Rider/issues/86). They are **issues, not deployed implementations**.
4. Capture live `/health`, redacted configuration, Stripe/CRA/provider evidence, actual device reports and market-admission status on an exact release SHA; follow `docs/COMMERCIAL-RELEASE-EVIDENCE.md`.
4. Activate covered counties **individually through atomic existing market gates** after verified shared/local evidence, not by changing a flag. Then observe production health, money, provider, dispatch, safety and Operator success from one phone.

## 5. How this ledger stays accurate across conversations

**Start-of-task protocol:** (0) Extract **settled founder decisions and already fixed/deployed issues** from this ledger, verify exact closed PRs against source, and do not present them as pending merely because an old screenshot or stale status block remains; (a) Read this file and `AGENTS.md`; (b) query GitHub `release/current` and current open PRs/issues; (c) verify Render's exact live SHA; (d) read live protected provider/market records when authorized; (e) inspect **relevant** implementation; (f) work on the shortest actual blocker; (g) report what changed and what was merely proposed. Do **not** ask again for settled decisions above.

**Update protocol:** Every PR that changes a settled decision, operational architecture, readiness gate, actual provider commissioning or real release evidence must update this ledger **in the same PR** (and create a superseding dated ADR if a founder decision changes). For ordinary bug-only PRs, update the last-deployed SHA/evidence only when independently verified. Keep the ledger short: add dated change entries and revise the current matrix, but leave PRs and Git history as the detail record. Never represent branch contents as production. No secrets, personal data, policy documents or screening reports in Git. Mark unverified claims openly and cite a code path, exact commit, official authority or restricted evidence reference.

**2026-10-10 checkpoints:** Canonical ledger established after reconciling PRs #73–#87; PR #90 subsequently repaired and deployed screening-renewal pagination/cases on exact SHA `24c0fbc4785f7644ae1e2e8055e1b750c8d0a828` with all six required workflow suites passing, and #88 was closed. Previous handoffs remain historical. Future releases supersede this status only with independent verification.

- [Apple Human Interface Guidelines](https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/design-principles); [Google SRE toil](https://sre.google/sre-book/eliminating-toil/); [GOV.UK cost-effective service standard](https://www.gov.uk/service-manual/service-standard/point-11-choose-the-right-tools-and-technology).**2026-10-11 next cost-audit correction — DO NOT CONFLATE COMPONENTS OR ASSUME WEEKLY PAYOUT:** The code uses MapLibre free renderer with OpenFreeMap no-fee commercial vector tiles, but no installed Stadia tile failover. Stadia's $20 Starter, $80 Standard and $250 Professional tiers are **alternatives with included credits**, not invoices or automatically switched backups. Self-hosted OSRM routing and OpenTripPlanner are separate from tiles. `backend/tolls.js` optimizes HERE billable requests only when `region.tollCoverage.certified === true`, but South Florida has `tollPolicy.status=verified` and NO equivalent tollCoverage.certified field; therefore HERE could still be called for supposedly clear routes, when configured. Do not assert an exceptional-only HERE expense without actual quote metrics and official facility certification; see issue #121. Existing Stripe US Express `accounts.create` does not configure payout schedules, and official Stripe Connect docs specify **daily rolling** payout as default, so the previous 4-weekly-payout cost scenario is not verified. Model 20 bank payouts/month per Operator working 20 days until live payout events/schedule prove otherwise; that is $5 versus the earlier $1 fixed monthly payout cost per active Operator, a $4 difference, independent of the $2 monthly active-account charge that the Founder approves recovering with forward-looking agreement. Cost scale must compare actual demand, hours, quote count, carrier costs and regional capacity (50/100/200/500/1000/10K/50K), not multiply registrations by fixed guesses. `backend/fleet-economics-forecast.js` corrected in this research PR; exact-head CI/deploy NOT yet certified. Founder is the only current administrator; full company insurance/legal/support costs cannot be set to zero by assumption, even when no staff salaries are paid. Research: https://docs.stripe.com/connect/payouts-connected-accounts, https://stadiamaps.com/pricing/, https://docs.here.com/routing/docs/routing-v8-tolls-for-route and https://openfreemap.org/tos/.
